# Saṃyutta Nikāya (SN 11)

> **Nikāya:** Saṃyuttanikāya
> **Saṃyutta:** SN 11
> **PTS Volume:** Saṃyutta Nikāya Vol. 1
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

<!-- PTS Page 001 -->
<a id="page-001"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 001]*

1 SAṂYUTTA-NIKĀYA. DIVISION I. -- SAGĀTHA.

BOOK I. -- DEVATĀ-SAṂYUTTA.

<p align="center">Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa ||</p>


CHAPTER I. NAḶAVAGGA.

SN_1.1,1.1. Oghaṃ. Evam me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||  
Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
Upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Kathaṃ nu tvaṃ mārisa ogham atarī ti || ||  
Appatiṭṭhaṃ khvāhaṃ {avuso} anāyūhaṃ ogham atarin ti || ||  
Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ mārisa appatiṭṭhaṃ anāyūhaṃ ogham atarīti || ||  
Yadā svāham āvuso santiṭṭhāmi tadāssu saṃsīdāmi ||  
yadā svāham āvuso āyūhāmi[^001-1] tadāssu nibbuyhāmi[^001-2] ||  
Evam khvāham āvuso appatiṭṭhaṃ anāyūhaṃ ogham atarin ti || ||  
Cirassaṃ vata passāmi ||  
brāhmaṇaṃ parinibbutaṃ ||  
appatiṭṭhaṃ anāyūhaṃ ||  
tiṇṇam loke visattikan ti || ||  
Idam avoca sā devatā ||  
samanuñño satthā ahosi || ||  
Atha kho sā devatā samanuñño[^001-3] me satthāti Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth-ev-antaradhāyīti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 002 -->
<a id="page-002"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 002]*

SN_1.1,1.2. Nimokkho. Sāvatthiyaṃ || ||  
Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Jānāsi no tvaṃ mārisa sattānaṃ nimokkhaṃ pamokkhaṃ vivekan ti || ||  
Jānāmi[^002-1] khvāham[^002-2] āvuso sattānam nimokkham pamokkhaṃ[^002-3] vivekan ti || ||  
Yathā katham pana tvaṃ mārisa jānāsi sattānaṃ nimokkhaṃ pamokkhaṃ vivekan ti || ||  
Nandī-bhava-parikkhayā ||  
saññā-viññāṇa-saṅkhayā ||  
vedanānaṃ nirodhā[^002-4] upasamā ||  
evaṃ khvāham āvuso jānāmi ||  
sattānaṃ nimokkhaṃ[^002-5] ||  
pamokkhaṃ vivekan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,1.3. Upaneyyam. Evam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Upanīyati jīvitam appam āyu ||  
jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā ||  
etaṃ bhayaṃ marane pekkhamāno ||  
puññāni kayirātha sukhāvahānī ti[^002-6] || ||  
Upanīyati jīvitam appam āyu ||  
jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā ||  
etaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇe pekkhamāno ||  
lokāmisaṃ pajahe santipekkho ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 003 -->
<a id="page-003"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 003]*

SN_1.1,1.4. Accenti. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Accenti[^003-1] kālā tarayanti rattiyo ||  
vayoguṇā anupubbaṃ jahanti ||  
etaṃ bhayaṃ {maraṇe} pekkhamāno ||  
puññāni kayirātha sukhāvahānīti || ||  
Accenti kālā tarayanti rattiyo ||  
vayoguṇā anupubbaṃ jahanti ||  
etaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇe pekkhamāno ||  
lokāmisaṃ pajahe santipekkho ti[^003-2] || ||  

SN_1.1,1.5. Kati chinde. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi[^003-3] || ||  
Kati chinde kati jahe ||  
kati vuttari bhāvaye ||  
katisaṅgātigo bhikkhu ||  
oghatiṇṇo ti viccatīti || ||  
Pañca chinde pañca jahe ||  
pañca vuttari bhāvaye ||  
pañcasaṅgātigo[^003-4] bhikkhu ||  
oghatiṇṇo ti vuccatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,1.6. Jāgaram. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi[^003-5] || ||  
Kati jāgarataṃ suttā ||  
kati suttesu jāgarā ||  
katīhi rajaṃ ādeti ||  
katīhi parisujjhatīti || ||  
Pañca jāgarataṃ suttā ||  
pañca-suttesu jāgarā ||  
pañcahi rajam ādeti ||  
pañcahi[^003-6] parisujjhatī ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 004 -->
<a id="page-004"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 004]*

SN_1.1,1.7. Appaṭividitā. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Yesaṃ dhammā appaṭividitā ||  
paravādesu nīyare ||  
suttā te nappabujjhanti ||  
kālo tesaṃ {pabujjhitun} ti || ||  
Yesaṃ dhammā suppaṭividitā[^004-1] ||  
paravādesu na nīyare[^004-2] ||  
{te sambuddhā} sammadaññā[^004-3] ||  
caranti visame saman ti || ||  

SN_1.1,1.8. Susammuṭṭhā Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Yesaṃ dhammā susammuṭṭhā ||  
paravādesu nīyare ||  
suttā te nappabujjhanti ||  
kālo tesaṃ pabujjhitun ti || ||  
Yesaṃ dhammā asammuṭṭhā ||  
paravādesu na nīyare ||  
te sambuddhā sammadaññā ||  
caranti visame saman ti[^004-4] || ||  

SN_1.1,1.9. Mānakāma. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Na mānakāmassa damo idh-atthi[^004-5] ||  
na monam atthi asamāhitassa ||  
eko araññe viharaṃ pamatto ||  
na maccudheyyassa tareyya[^004-6] pāran ti[^004-7] || ||  
Mānam pahāya susamāhitatto ||  
sucetaso sabbadhi vippamutto[^004-8] ||  
eko araññe viharaṃ appamatto ||  
sa maccudheyyassa tareyya pāran ti[^004-9] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 005 -->
<a id="page-005"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 005]*

SN_1.1,1.10. Araññe. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Araññe viharantānaṃ ||  
santānaṃ brahmacārinaṃ ||  
ekabhattaṃ bhuñjamānānaṃ ||  
kena vaṇṇo pasīdatīti[^005-1] || ||  
Atītaṃ nānusocanti ||  
nappajappanti nāgataṃ[^005-2] ||  
paccuppannena yāpenti ||  
tena vaṇṇo pasīdati || ||  
anāgatappajappāya ||  
atītassānusocanā ||  
etena bālā sussanti ||  
naḷo va harito luto ti || ||  
Naḷavaggo pathamo ||  
Tatr-uddānaṃ ||  
Ogham Nimokkho Upaneyyaṃ || Accenti Katichindi ca ||  
Jāgaram Appaṭividitā ||  
Susammutthā Māna-kāminā ||  
Araññe dasamo vutto ||  
vaggo tena pavuccati || ||  

___________________________________________

CHAPTER II. NANDANA-VAGGA.

SN_1.1,2.1. Nandana. Evaṃ me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme ||  
Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || Bhikkhavo ti ||  
||  
Bhadante[^005-3] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ || ||  
Bhagavā etad avoca || ||  
Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave aññatarā Tāvatiṃsa-kāyikā devatā Nandanavane[^005-4] accharāsaṅghaparivutā dibbehi pañcakāmaguṇehi samappitā samaṅgibhūtā paricāriyamānā[^005-5] tāyaṃ velāyam imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Na te sukhaṃ pajānanti ||  
ye na passanti Nandanaṃ ||  
āvāsaṃ naradevānaṃ ||  
tidasānaṃ yasassinan ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 006 -->
<a id="page-006"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 006]*

Evaṃ vutte bhikkhave aññatarā devatā taṃ devataṃ gāthāya paccabhāsi[^006-1] || ||  
Na tvaṃ bāle pajānāsi[^006-2] ||  
yathā arahataṃ vaco ||  
aniccā sabba[^006-3] saṅkhārā ||  
uppādavayadhammino ||  
uppajjitvā nirujjhanti ||  
tesaṃ vūpasamo sukho ti || ||  

SN_1.1,2.2. Nandati. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Nandati puttehi puttimā ||  
gomiko[^006-4] gohi tath-eva nandati ||  
upadhīhi narassa nandanā ||  
na hi so nandati yo nirupadhīti || ||  
Socati puttehi puttimā ||  
gomiko gohi tath-eva socati ||  
upadhīhi narassa socanā ||  
na hi socati yo nirupadhīti || ||  

SN_1.1,2.3. Natthi puttasamam. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi. || ||  
Natthi puttasamaṃ pemaṃ ||  
natthi gosamitam dhanaṃ ||  
natthi suriyasamā ābhā. ||  
samudda[^006-5] paramā sarāti || ||  
Natthi attasamaṃ pemaṃ ||  
natthi dhaññasamaṃ dhanaṃ ||  
natthi paññāsamā ābhā ||  
vuṭṭhi ve paramā sarā ti || ||  

SN_1.1,2.4. Khattiyo. Khattiyo dvipadaṃ[^006-6] seṭṭho ||  
balivaddo[^006-7] catuppadaṃ ||  
kumārī[^006-8] seṭṭhā bhariyānaṃ ||  
yo ca puttānaṃ pubbajoti || ||  
Sambuddho dvipadaṃ seṭṭho ||  
ājānīyo catuppadaṃ ||  
sussūsā seṭṭhā bhariyānaṃ ||  
yo ca puttānam assavo ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 007 -->
<a id="page-007"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 007]*

SN_1.1,2.5. Sakamāno (or Santikāya). Ṭhite majjhantike kāle ||  
sannisinnesu[^007-1] pakkhisu ||  
saṇate va[^007-2] mahāraññaṃ[^007-3] ||  
taṃ bhayaṃ paṭibhāti manti || ||  
Ṭhite majjhantike kāle ||  
sannisinnesu[^007-4] pakkhisu ||  
saṇate va mahāraññaṃ ||  
sā ratī paṭibhāti man ti[^007-5] || ||  

SN_1.1,2.6. Niddā tandi. Niddā tandī vijambhikā[^007-6] ||  
aratī bhattasammado ||  
etena nappakāsati ||  
ariyamaggo idha pāṇinan ti || ||  
Niddaṃ tandiṃ vijambhikaṃ ||  
aratiṃ[^007-7] bhattasammadaṃ ||  
viriyena naṃ paṇāmetvā ||  
ariyamaggo visujjhatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,2.7. Dukkaraṃ (or Kummo). Dukkaraṃ duttitikkhañca[^007-8] ||  
avyattena[^007-9] ca sāmaññaṃ ||  
bahū hi tattha sambādhā ||  
yattha bālo visīdatīti || ||  
Kati-haṃ careyya sāmaññaṃ ||  
cittaṃ ce na nivāreyya[^007-10] ||  
pade pade visīdeyya ||  
saṅkappānaṃ vasānugo[^007-11] || ||  
Kummo va aṅgāni[^007-12] sake kapāle ||  
samodahaṃ bhikkhu mano-vitakke ||  
anissito aññam aheṭhayāno[^007-13] ||  
parinibbuto na upavadeyya kañcīti[^007-14] || ||  

SN_1.1,2.8. Hirī. Hirīnisedho puriso ||  
koci lokasmiṃ vijjati ||  
yo nindam appabodhati ||  
asso bhadro kasām ivā ti || ||  
Hirīnisedhā tanuyā[^007-15] ||  
ye caranti sadā satā ||  
antaṃ dukkhassa pappuyya[^007-16] ||  
caranti visame saman ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 008 -->
<a id="page-008"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 008]*

SN_1.1,2.9. Kuṭikā. Kacci te kuṭikā natthi ||  
kacci natthi kulāvakā ||  
kacci santānakā natthe ||  
kacci mutto-si bandhanā ti[^008-1] || ||  
Taggha me kuṭikā natthi ||  
taggha natthi kulāvakā ||  
taggha santānakā natthi ||  
taggha mutto-mhi bandhanā ti[^008-2] || ||  
Kintāhaṃ kuṭikam brūmi ||  
kinte brūmi kulāvakaṃ[^008-3] ||  
kinte santānakaṃ[^008-4] brūmi ||  
kintāhaṃ brūmi[^008-5] bandhananti ||  
Mātaraṃ kuṭikam brūsi ||  
bhariyaṃ brūsi kulāvakaṃ ||  
putte santānake brūsi ||  
taṇham[^008-6] me brūsi[^008-7] bandhanan ti || ||  
sāhu te kuṭikā natthi ||  
sāhu natthi kulāvakā ||  
sāhu santānakā natthi ||  
sāhu mutto si bandhanā ti[^008-8] || ||  

SN_1.1,2.10. Samiddhi.9

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Tapodārāme || ||  

2. Atha kho āyasmā Samiddhi rattiyā paccusa-samayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya yena Tapodā ten-upasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ ||  
Tapode gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi gattāni sukkhāpayamāno[^008-10] ||  

3. Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Tapodam obhāsetvā yen'; āyasmā Samiddhi ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā vehāsaṃ ṭhitā[^008-11] āyasmantaṃ Samiddhiṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Abhutvā bhikkhasi bhikkhu ||  
na hi bhutvāna bhikkhasi ||  
bhutvāna bhikkhu bhikkhassu ||  
mā taṃ kālo upaccagāti.[^008-12] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 009 -->
<a id="page-009"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 009]*

Kālaṃ vo-haṃ na jānāmi ||  
channo kālo na dissati ||  
tasmā abhutvā bhikkhāmi ||  
mā maṃ kālo upaccagāti || ||  

4. Atha kho sā devatā pathaviyam patiṭṭhahitvā āyasmantam Samiddhim etad avoca[^009-1] || ||  
Daharo tvaṃ bhikkhu pabbajito susu kālakeso bhadrena[^009-2] yobbanena samannāgato pathamena vayasā anikiḷitāvī[^009-3] kāmesu ||  
Bhuñja bhikkhu mānusake kāme mā sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikam anudhāvī ti[^009-4] || ||  

5. Na khvāham āvuso sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikam anudhavāmi ||  
Kālikañca[^009-5] khvāham āvuso hitvā sandiṭṭhikam anudhāvāmi ||  
Kālikā hi āvuso kāmā vuttā Bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyasā ādīnavo ettha bhīyo ||  
Sandiṭṭhiko ayaṃ dhammo akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^009-6] paccattaṃ[^009-7] veditabbo viññūhīti || ||  

6. Kathañca bhikkhu kālikā[^009-8] kāmā vuttā Bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā ādīnavo ettha bhīyo[^009-9] ||  
Kathaṃ sandiṭṭhiko ayaṃ dhammo akāliko[^009-10] ehipassiko opanayiko paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhīti || ||  

7. Ahaṃ kho āvuso navo acirapabbajito adhunāgato ||  
imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ na khvāhaṃ[^009-11] sakkomi vitthārena ācikkhituṃ ||  
Ayaṃ so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho Rājagahe viharati Tapodārāme ||  
Taṃ Bhagavantam upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccha[^009-12] ||  
Yathā te Bhagavā vyākaroti tathā naṃ dhāreyyāsīti || ||  

8. Na kho bhikkhu sukaro so Bhagavā amhehi upasaṅkamitum aññāhi mahesakkhahi[^009-13] devatāhi parivuto ||  
Sa ce kho tvaṃ bhikkhu taṃ[^009-14] Bhagavantam upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ pucceyyāsi mayam pi āgaccheyyāma dhammasavanāyā ti || ||  

9. Evam āvuso ti kho āyasmā Samiddhi tassā devatāya paṭisutvā[^009-15] yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
Upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 010 -->
<a id="page-010"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 010]*

Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Samiddhi Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca || ||  

10. Idhāhaṃ bhante rattiyā paccusasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya yena Tapodā ten-upasaṅkamiṃ gattāni parisiñcituṃ ||  
Tapode gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsiṃ gattāni sukkhāpayamāno ||  
Atha kho bhante aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Tapodaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā vehāsaṃ ṭhitā[^010-1] imāya gāthāya ajjhabhāsi ||  
Abhutvā bhikkhasi bhikkhu ||  
na hi bhutvāna bhikkhasi ||  
bhutvāna bhikkhu bhikkhassu ||  
mā taṃ kālo upaccagā ti || ||  

11. Evaṃ vutte aham[^010-2] bhante taṃ devataṃ gāthāya paccabhāsiṃ[^010-3] || ||  
Kālaṃ vo-haṃ na jānāmi ||  
channo[^010-4] kālo na dissati ||  
tasmā abhutvā bhikkhāmi ||  
mā maṃ kālo upaccagā ti || ||  

12. Atha kho bhante sā devatā pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhahitvā mam etad avoca || ||  
Daharo tvam bhikkhu pabbajito susu[^010-5] kālakeso[^010-6] bhadrena yobbanena samannāgato pathamena vayasā anikīḷitāvī[^010-7] kāmesu ||  
Bhuñja bhikkhu mānusake kāme mā sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikam anudhāvī ti || ||  

13. Evaṃ vutte-haṃ[^010-8] bhante taṃ devatam etad avocaṃ || ||  
Na khvāhaṃ[^010-9] āvuso sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvāmi ||  
kālikaṃ ca khvāhaṃ āvuso hitvā sandiṭṭhikam anudhāvāmi ||  
Kālikā hi āvuso kāmā vuttā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā ādīnavo ettha bhīyo ||  
Sandiṭṭhiko ayam dhammo akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko paccattam veditabbo viññūhīti || ||  

14. Evaṃ vutte bhante sā devatā mam etad avoca ||  
Kathaṃ ca bhikkhu kālikā kāmā vuttā Bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā ādīnavo ettha bhīyo ||  
Kathaṃ san-ḥ


<!-- PTS Page 011 -->
<a id="page-011"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 011]*

diṭṭhiko ayaṃ dhammo akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhīti || ||  

15. Evaṃ vutte-haṃ bhante[^011-1] taṃ devatam etad avocaṃ || ||  
Aham kho āvuso navo acirapabbajito adhunāgato imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ na khvāham[^011-2] sakkomi vitthārena ācikkhituṃ ||  
ayaṃ so[^011-3] Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho Rājagahe viharati Tapodārāme ||  
taṃ Bhagavantam upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccha[^011-4] ||  
yathā te Bhagavā vyākaroti tathā naṃ dhāreyyāsīti || ||  

16. Evaṃ vutte bhante sā devatā maṃ etad avoca ||  
Na kho bhikkhu sukaro so Bhagavā amhehi[^011-5] upasaṅkamitum aññāhi mahesakkhāhi devatāhi parivuto ||  
Sace kho tvaṃ bhikkhu taṃ[^011-6] Bhagavantam upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyāsi[^011-7] mayam pi āgaccheyyāma dhammasavanāyāti ||  
Sace bhante tassā[^011-8] devatāya saccaṃ vacanaṃ idheva sā devatā avidūre ti || ||  

17. Evaṃ vutte sā devatā āyasmantaṃ Samiddhim etad avoca ||  
Puccha bhikkhu puccha bhikkhu yam[^011-9] aham anuppattoti[^011-10] || ||  

18. Atha kho Bhagavā taṃ[^011-11] devataṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Akkheyyasaññino sattā ||  
akkheyyasmiṃ patiṭṭhitā ||  
akkheyyam apariññāya ||  
yogam āyanti maccuno || ||  
akkheyyañ ca pariññāya[^011-12] ||  
akkhātāraṃ[^011-13] na maññati ||  
taṃ hi tassa na hotīti ||  
yena naṃ[^011-14] vajjā na tassa atthi[^011-15] || ||  
Sace vijānāsi[^011-16] vadehi yakkhīti[^011-17] || ||  

19. Na khvāham bhante imassa Bhagavatā saṅkhittena bhāsitāssa vitthārena attham ājānāmi[^011-18] ||  
Sādhu me[^011-19] bhante Bhagavā tathā[^011-20] bhāsatu yathāham imassa Bhagavatā saṅkhittena bhāsitassa vitthārena atthaṃ jāneyyan ti[^011-21] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 012 -->
<a id="page-012"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 012]*

20. Samo visesī athavā nihīno[^012-1] ||  
yo maññati so vivadetha tena ||  
tīsu vidhāsu avikampamāno ||  
samo visesīti na[^012-2] tassa hoti || ||  
Sace vijānāsi vadehi yakkhīti[^012-3] || ||  

21. Imassa pi khvāhaṃ bhante Bhagavatā saṅkhittena bhāsitassa na vitthārena attham ājānāmi[^012-4] ||  
Sādhu me[^012-5] bhante Bhagavā tathā bhāsatu yathāham imassa Bhagavatā saṅkhittena bhāsitassa vitthārena atthaṃ jāneyyan ti[^012-6] || ||  

22. Pahāsi saṅkhaṃ[^012-7] na vimānam ajjhagā[^012-8] ||  
acchecchi[^012-9] taṇham idha nāmarūpe ||  
tam chinnagandham anighaṃ nirāsam[^012-10] ||  
pariyesamānā nājjhāgamuṃ ||  
devā manussā idha vā huraṃ vā ||  
saggesu vā sabbanivesanesu[^012-11] || ||  
Sace vijānāsi vadehi yakkhīti[^012-12] ||  

23. Imassa khvāhaṃ bhante Bhagavatā saṅkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ[^012-13] vitthārena attham ājānāmi[^012-14] ||  
Pāpaṃ na kayirā[^012-15] vacasā manasā ||  
kāyena vā[^012-16] kiñcana sabbaloke ||  
kāme pahāya satimā sampajāno ||  
dukkhaṃ na sevetha anatthasaṃhitan ti[^012-17] || ||  
Nandana-vaggo dutiyo || ||  
Tatr-uddānam ||  
Nandanā Nandati c-eva || Natthiputtasamena ca ||  
Khattiyo Sakamāno ca[^012-18] ||  
Niddātandi ca Dukkaraṃ[^012-19] ||  
Hirī Kuṭikā navamo ||  
dasamo vutto Samiddhināti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 013 -->
<a id="page-013"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 013]*

CHAPTER III. SATTI-VAGGO. Sāvatthi nidānam || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi[^013-1] || ||  

SN_1.1,3.1. Sattiyā. Sattiyā viya omaṭṭho ||  
ḍayhamāne[^013-2] va matthake ||  
kāmarāgappahānāya[^013-3] ||  
sato bhikkhu paribbaje ti[^013-4] || ||  
Sattiyā viya omaṭṭho ||  
ḍayhamāne va matthake ||  
sakkāyadiṭṭhippahānāya[^013-5] ||  
sato bhikkhu paribbaje ti || ||  

SN_1.1,3.2. Phusati. Nāphusantam phusati ca[^013-6] ||  
phusantaṃ ca tato phuse ||  
tasmā phusantam phusati ||  
appaduṭṭhapadosinan ti[^013-7] || ||  
Yo appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati ||  
suddhassa posassa anaṅgaṇassa[^013-8] ||  
tam eva bālaṃ pacceti pāpaṃ ||  
sukhumo rajo paṭivātaṃ va khitto ti[^013-9] || ||  

SN_1.1,3.3. Jaṭā. Antojaṭā bahijaṭā ||  
jaṭāya jaṭitā pajā ||  
taṃ taṃ Gotama pucchāmi ||  
ko imaṃ vijaṭaye jaṭan ti || ||  
Sīle patiṭṭhāya naro sapañño ||  
cittaṃ paññañca bhāvayaṃ ||  
ātāpī nipako bhikkhu ||  
so imaṃ vijaṭaye jaṭan ti || ||  
yesaṃ rāgo ca doso ca ||  
avijjā ca virājitā ||  
khīṇāsavā arahanto ||  
tesaṃ vijaṭitā jaṭā || ||  
yattha nāmañca rūpañca ||  
asesam uparujjhati ||  
paṭighaṃ rūpasaññā ca ||  
ettha sā chijjate[^013-10] jaṭāti[^013-11] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 014 -->
<a id="page-014"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 014]*

SN_1.1,3.4. Mano-nivāraṇā. Yato yato mano[^014-1] nivāraye ||  
na dukkham eti naṃ tato tato ||  
sa sabbato mano nivāraye ||  
sa sabbato dukkhā pamuccati || ||  
Na sabbato mano nivāraye ||  
na[^014-2] mano sayatattam[^014-3] āgataṃ[^014-4] ||  
yato yato ca[^014-5] pāpakaṃ ||  
tato tato mano nivāraye ti || ||  

SN_1.1,3.5. Arahaṃ. Yo hoti bhikkhu arahaṃ katāvī ||  
khīṇāsavo[^014-6] antimadehadhārī ||  
ahaṃ vadāmīti pi so vadeyya ||  
mamaṃ vadantīti[^014-7] pi so[^014-8] vadeyya[^014-9] || ||  
[Yo hoti bhikkhu arahaṃ katāvī ||  
khīṇāsavo antimadehadhārī ||  
ahaṃ vadāmīti pi so vadeyya ||  
mamaṃ vadantīti pi so vadeyya ||  
loke samaññaṃ kusalo viditvā ||  
vohāramattena so vohareyyāti[^014-10]] || ||  
Yo hoti bhikkhu arahaṃ katāvī ||  
khīṇāsavo antimadehadhārī ||  
mānaṃ nu kho so[^014-11] upāgamma bhikkhu ||  
ahaṃ vadāmmīti pi so vadeyya ||  
mamaṃ vadantīti pi so vadeyyāti || ||  
Pahīnamānassa na santi ganthā[^014-12] ||  
vidhūpitā mānaganthassa[^014-13] sabbe ||  
Sa vītivatto yamataṃ sumedho ||  


<!-- PTS Page 015 -->
<a id="page-015"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 015]*

ahaṃ vadāmīti pi so vadeyya ||  
[mamaṃ vadantīti pi so vadeyya] [^015-1] ||  
loke samaññaṃ kusalo viditvā vohāramattena so vohareyyāti || ||  

SN_1.1,3.6. Pajjoto. Kati lokasmiṃ pajjotā[^015-2] ||  
yehi loko[^015-3] pakāsati. ||  
bhavantaṃ[^015-4] puṭṭhum āgamma ||  
kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayan ti || ||  
Cattāro loke[^015-5] pajjotā ||  
pañcam-etthana vijjati[^015-6] ||  
divā tapati ādicco ||  
rattim ābhāti candimā || ||  
atha aggi divārattiṃ ||  
tattha tattha pabhāsati[^015-7] ||  
sambuddho tapataṃ seṭṭho ||  
esā ābhā anuttarā ti || ||  

SN_1.1,3.7. Sarā. Kuto sarā nivattanti ||  
kattha[^015-8] vaṭṭam na vaṭṭati[^015-9] ||  
kattha nāmañca rūpañca ||  
asesam uparujjhatīti || ||  
Yattha āpo ca pathavī ||  
tejo vāyo na gādhati ||  
ato sarā nivattanti ||  
ettha vaṭṭam na vaṭṭati ||  
ettha nāmañca rupañca ||  
asesam uparujjhatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,3.8. Mahaddhana. Mahaddhanā mahābhogā ||  
raṭṭhavanto pi khattiyā ||  
aññamaññābhigijjhanti ||  
kāmesu analaṅkatā || ||  
tesu ussukkajātesu ||  
bhavasotānusārisu ||  
gedhataṃham[^015-10] pajahiṃsu[^015-11] ||ke lokasmim anussukkāti || ||  
Hitvā agāram pabbajitvā ||  
hitvā puttaṃ pasuṃ piyaṃ[^015-12] ||  
hitvā rāgañca dosañca ||  
avijjañca virājiya[^015-13] ||  
khīṇāsavā arahanto ||  
te[^015-14] lokasmiṃ anussukā ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 016 -->
<a id="page-016"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 016]*

SN_1.1,3.9. Catucakka. Catucakkam navadvāraṃ ||  
puṇṇam lobhena[^016-1] saṃyutaṃ ||  
paṅkajātaṃ mahāvīra ||  
kathaṃ yātrā bhavissatīti[^016-2] || ||  
Chetvā nandiṃ varattañca[^016-3] ||  
icchālobhañca pāpakaṃ ||  
samūlaṃ taṇham abbuyha ||  
evaṃ yātrā bhavissatīti[^016-4] || ||  

SN_1.1,3.10. Enijaṅgha. Enijaṅghaṃ kisaṃ vīraṃ ||  
appāhāram alolupaṃ ||  
sīhaṃ v-ekacaraṃ nāgam ||kāmesu anapekkhinaṃ ||  
upasaṅkamma pucchāma[^016-5] ||  
kathaṃ dukkhā pamuccatīti || ||  
Pañcakāmaguṇā loke ||  
mano chaṭṭhā paveditā ||  
ettha chandaṃ virājetvā ||  
evaṃ dukkhā pamuccatīti || ||  
Satti-vaggo tatiyo || ||  
Tatr-uddānam ||  
Sattiyā Phusati c-eva ||  
Jaṭā Manonivāraṇā || Arahantena Pajjoto ||  
Sarā Mahaddhanena ca ||  
Catucakkena navamaṃ ||  
Enijaṅghena te dasāti || ||

CHAPTER IV. SATULLAPAKĀYIKA-VAGGA

SN_1.1,4.1. Sabbhi.

1. Evaṃ me sutaṃ Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme ||  

2. Atha kho sambahulā Satullapakayikā[^016-6] devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā ||  
yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu ||  


<!-- PTS Page 017 -->
<a id="page-017"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 017]*

3. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ[^017-1] ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññāya ||  
seyyo hoti na pāpiyo ti || ||  

4. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi. || ||  
Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññāya ||  
paññā labbhati[^017-2] nāññato ti || ||  

5. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi ||  
Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññāya ||  
soka-majjhe na socatīti || ||  

6. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññaya ||  
ñāti-majjhe virocatīti ||  

7. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññāya ||  
sattā gacchanti suggatin ti[^017-3] || ||  

8. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññāya ||  
sattā tiṭṭhanti sātatan ti || ||  

9. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavatam etad avoca || ||  
Kassa nu kho Bhagavā subhāsitan ti || ||  
Sabbāsaṃ vo subhāsitaṃ pariyāyena ||  
api ca mamam pi[^017-4] suṇātha || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 018 -->
<a id="page-018"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 018]*

Sabbhir eva samāsetha ||  
sabbhi kubbetha santhavaṃ ||  
sataṃ saddhammam aññāya[^018-1] ||  
sabbadukkhā pamuccatīti[^018-2] || ||  

SN_1.1,4.2. Macchari.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||  

2. Atha kho sambahulā Satullapakāyikā devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu. ||  

3. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Maccherā ca pamādā ca ||  
evaṃ {dānaṃ} na dīyati ||  
puññam ākaṅkhamānena ||  
deyyaṃ hoti vijānatā ti || ||  

4. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imā gāthayo[^018-3] abhāsi || ||  
Yass-eva bhīto na dadāti maccharī ||  
tad evādādato[^018-4] bhayaṃ ||  
jighacchā ca pipāsā ca ||  
yassa bhāyati maccharī ||  
tam eva bālaṃ phusati ||  
asmiṃ loke paramhi ca || ||  
Tasmā vineyya maccheraṃ ||  
dajjā dānaṃ malābhibhū ||  
puññāni paralokasmiṃ ||  
patiṭṭhā honti pāṇinan ti || ||  

5. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi ||  
Te matesu na mīyanti ||  
panthānaṃ va sagāvajjaṃ[^018-5] ||  
appasmiṃ ye pavecchanti ||  
esa dhammo sanantano || ||  
appasm-eke pavecchanti ||  
bahun-eke[^018-6] na dicchare ||  
appasmā dakkhiṇā dinnā ||  
sahassena samaṃ mitā ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 019 -->
<a id="page-019"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 019]*

6. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imā gāṭhāyo[^019-1] abhāsi ||  
Duddadaṃ dadamānānaṃ ||  
dukkaraṃ kamma kubbataṃ ||  
asanto nānukubbanti ||  
sataṃ dhammo durannayo[^019-2] || ||  
Tasmā satañca asatañca[^019-3] ||  
nānā hoti ito gati ||  
asanto nirayaṃ yanti ||  
santo saggaparāyanā ti[^019-4] || ||  

7. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavantam[^019-5] etad avoca ||  
Kassa nu kho Bhagavā subhāsitan ti || ||  
Sabbāsaṃ vo subhāsitaṃ pariyāyena ||  
api mamam pi[^019-6] suṇātha || ||  
Dhammaṃ care yo samucchakaṃ[^019-7] care ||  
dāram ca posaṃ dadam appakasmiṃ ||  
sataṃ sahassānaṃ sahassayāginaṃ ||  
kalam pi[^019-8] nāgghanti tathāvidhassa te ti || ||  

8. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavantaṃ gāthaya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Ken-esaṃ[^019-9] yañño vipulo mahaggato ||  
samena dinnassa na aggham eti ||  
sataṃ[^019-10] sahassānaṃ sahassayāginaṃ ||  
kalam pi nāgghanti tathāvidhassa te ti || ||  

9. Atha kho Bhagavā taṃ {devataṃ,} gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Dadanti eke[^019-11] visame[^019-12] niviṭṭhā ||  
chetvā[^019-13] vadhitvā atha socayitvā ||  
sā dakkhiṇā assumukhā sadaṇḍā ||  
samena dinnassa na aggham eti || ||  
Evaṃ[^019-14] sahassānaṃ sahassayāginam ||  
kalam pi[^019-15] nāgghanti tathāvidhassa te ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 020 -->
<a id="page-020"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 020]*

SN_1.1,4.3. Sādhu.

1. Sāvatthi ārāme || ||  

2. Atha kho sambahulā Satullapakāyikā devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu || ||  

3. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Sādhu[^020-1] kho mārisa dānaṃ || ||  
Maccherā ca pamādā ca ||  
evaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati ||  
puññam ākaṅkhamānena ||  
deyyaṃ hoti vijānatā ti || ||  

4. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Sādhu kho mārisa dānaṃ ||  
api ca appasmim pi sādhu[^020-2] dānaṃ || Appasm-eke pavecchanti ||  
bahun-eke[^020-3] na dicchare ||  
appasmā dakkhiṇā dinnā ||  
sahassena samam mitā ti[^020-4] || ||  

5. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udanesi || ||  
Sādhu kho mārisa dānaṃ ||  
Appasmim pi sādhu dānam ||  
Api ca saddhāya pi sādhu dānam ||  
Dānañca yuddhañca samānam āhu ||  
Appāpi santā bahuke jinanti ||  
Appam pi ce saddahāno dadāti ||  
ten-eva so hoti sukhī paratthā ti[^020-5] || ||  

6. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Sādhu kho mārisa dānaṃ ||  
appasmiṃ pi sādhu dānaṃ ||  


<!-- PTS Page 021 -->
<a id="page-021"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 021]*

saddhāya pi sādhu[^021-1] dānam ||  
api ca dhammaladdhassa pi sādhu dānaṃ || ||  
Yo dhammaladdhassa dadāti dānam ||  
uṭṭhānaviriyādhigatassa jantu ||  
atikkamma so vetaraṇiṃ Yamassa ||  
dibbāni ṭhānāni upeti macco ti || ||  

7. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Sādhu kho mārisa dānaṃ ||  
Appasmiṃ pi sādhu dānaṃ ||  
Saddhāya pi sādhu dānaṃ ||  
Dhammaladdhassa pi sādhu dānam ||  
Api ca viceyyadānam pi sādhu[^021-2] || ||  
Viceyyadānaṃ sugatappasatthaṃ[^021-3] ||  
ye dakkhiṇeyyā idha jīvaloke ||  
etesu dinnāni mahapphallāni ||  
bījāni vuttāni[^021-4] yathā sukhette ti || ||  

8. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Sādhu kho mārisa dānam ||  
Appasmim pi sādhu dānaṃ ||  
Saddhāya pi sādhu dānaṃ ||  
Dhammaladdhassa pi sādhu dānaṃ ||  
Viceyyadānam pi sādhu[^021-5] ||  
Api ca pāṇesu ca[^021-6] sādhu saṃyamo || ||  
Yo pāṇabhūtesu[^021-7] aheṭhayaṃ[^021-8] caraṃ ||  
parūpavādā na karoti pāpaṃ ||  
bhīruṃ[^021-9] pasaṃsanti na hi tattha sūraṃ ||  
bhayā hi santo na karonti pāpan ti || ||  

9. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 022 -->
<a id="page-022"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 022]*

Kassā nu kho Bhagavā subhāsitan ti || ||  
Sabbāsaṃ vo subhāsitaṃ pariyāyena ||  
api mamam pi[^022-1] suṇātha || ||  
Saddhāhi[^022-2] dānam bahudhā[^022-3] pasatthaṃ ||  
dānā ca[^022-4] kho dhammapadaṃ va[^022-5] seyyo || ||  
pubbeva hi pubbatareva santo ||  
nibbānam ev-ajjhagamuṃ sapaññā[^022-6] || ||  

SN_1.1,4.4. Na santi.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme ||  

2. Atha kho sambahulā Satullapakāyikā devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu ||  

3. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Na santi kāmā manujesu niccā ||  
santīdha[^022-7] kamanīyāni yesu baddho[^022-8] ||  
yesu pamatto[^022-9] apunāgamanaṃ ||  
anāgantvā puriso[^022-10] maccudheyyā ti || ||  
Chandajam aghaṃ ||  
chandajaṃ dukkhaṃ ||  
chandavinayā aghavinayo ||  
aghavinayā dukkhavinayo ti || ||  
Na te kāmā yāni citrāni[^022-11] loke ||  
saṅkapparāgo purisassa kāmo ||  
tiṭṭhanti citrāni tath-eva loke ||  
ath-ettha dhīrā vinayanti chandam || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 023 -->
<a id="page-023"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 023]*

Kodhaṃ jahe vippajaheyya mānaṃ ||  
saṃyojanaṃ sabbam atikkameyya ||  
taṃ nāmarūpasmim asajjamānaṃ ||  
akiñcanaṃ nānupatanti dukkhā[^023-1] || ||  
Pahāsi saṅkhaṃ[^023-2] na vimānam ajjhagā[^023-3] ||  
acchecchi[^023-4] taṇham idha nāmarūpe ||  
taṃ[^023-5] chinnagantham[^023-6] anighaṃ nirāsaṃ ||  
pariyesamānā na ca ajjhagamuṃ[^023-7] ||  
devā manussā idha vā huraṃ vā ||  
saggesu vā[^023-8] sabbanivesanesū ti[^023-9] || ||  
Taṃ ce hi nāddakkhuṃ[^023-10] tathā vimuttaṃ ||  
iccāyasmā Mogharājā ||  
deva manussā idha vā huraṃ vā ||  
naruttamaṃ atthacaraṃ narānaṃ ||  
ye taṃ namassanti pasaṃsiyā te ti || ||  
Pasaṃsiyā te pi bhavanti bhikkhu[^023-11] ||  
Mogharājā ti Bhagavā ||  
ye taṃ namassanti tathā vimuttam ||  
aññāya dhammaṃ vicikicchaṃ pahāya ||  
saṅgātigā[^023-12] te pi[^023-13] bhavanti bhikkhū ti || ||  

SN_1.1,4.5. Ujjhānasaññino.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme ||  

2. Atha kho sambahulā Ujjhānasaññikā devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu. ||  
Upasaṅkamitvā vekāsam aṭṭhaṃsu ||  


<!-- PTS Page 024 -->
<a id="page-024"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 024]*

3. Vehāsaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Aññathā santam attānam ||  
aññathā yo pavedaye[^024-1] ||  
nikacca kitavass-eva ||  
bhuttaṃ theyyena[^024-2] tassa taṃ || ||  
yaṃ hi kayirā taṃ hi vade ||  
yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vade ||  
akarontam bhāsamānānaṃ[^024-3] ||  
parijānanti paṇḍitā ti || ||  
Na yidam[^024-4] bhāsitamattena ||  
ekantasavanena vā ||  
anukkamituṃ ve[^024-5] sakkā ||  
yāyaṃ paṭipadā daḷhā[^024-6] ||  
yāya[^024-7] dhīrā pamuccanti ||  
jhāyino mārabandhanā || ||  
Na ve dhīrā pakubbanti ||  
viditvā lokapariyāyaṃ ||  
aññāya nibbutā dhīrā ||  
tiṇṇā loke visattikan-ti. || ||  

4. Atha kho tā devatāyo pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhahitvā Bhagavato pādesu sirasā nipatitvā Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || ||  
Accayo[^024-8] no bhante accagamā ||  
yathā bālā yatha mūḷhā yathā akusalā yā mayaṃ Bhagavantaṃ asādetabbaṃ[^024-9] amaññimhā ||  
tāsaṃ no[^024-10] bhante Bhagavā accayam accayato patigaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā ti || ||  

5. Atha kho Bhagavā sitaṃ pātvākāsi || ||  

6. Atha kho tā devatāyo bhiyyosomattāya ujjhāyantiyo vehāsam abbhuggañchuṃ[^024-11] || ||  

7. Ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imam gātham avoca || ||  
Accayaṃ desayantīnaṃ ||  
yo ve[^024-12] na patigaṇhati ||  
kopantaro dosagaru ||  
sa veraṃ paṭimuccatīti || ||  
Accayo ce na vijjetha[^024-13] ||  
no cīdhāpagatam[^024-14] siyā ||  
verāni na[^024-15] ca sammeyyuṃ ||  
kenīdha[^024-16] kusalo siyāti || ||  
Kass-accayā na vijjanti ||  
kassa natthi apagataṃ[^024-17] ||  
ko na sammoham āpādi ||  
ko ca[^024-18] dhīro sadā sato ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 025 -->
<a id="page-025"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 025]*

Tathāgatassa buddhassa ||  
sabbabhūtānukampino ||  
tass[^025-1]-accayā na vijjanti ||  
tassa natthi apagataṃ[^025-2] ||  
so na sammoham āpādi ||  
so ca[^025-3] dhīro sadā sato ti || ||  
Accayaṃ desayantīnaṃ ||  
yo ce na patigaṇhati ||  
kopantaro dosagaru ||  
yaṃ veraṃ[^025-4] paṭimuccati ||  
taṃ veraṃ nābhinandāmi ||  
paṭigaṇhāmi vo-ccayan ti[^025-5] || ||  

SN_1.1,4.6. Saddhā.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme ||  

2. Atha kho sambahulā Satullapakāyikā devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhasetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu ||  

3. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Saddhā dutiyā purisassa hoti ||  
no ce assaddhiyam[^025-6] avatiṭṭhati[^025-7] ||  
yaso ca kittī ca tatvassa hoti[^025-8] ||  
saggaṃ ca so gacchati sarīram pahāyā ti || ||  
Kodhaṃ jahe vippajaheyya mānaṃ ||  
saṃyojanaṃ sabbam atikkameyya ||  
taṃ nāmarūpasmim asajjamānaṃ ||  
akiñcanaṃ nānupatanti saṅgā ti[^025-9] ||  
Pamādam anuyuñjanti ||  
bālā dummedhino janā ||  
appamādaṃ ca medhāvī ||  
dhanaṃ seṭṭham va rakkhati || ||  
Mā pamādam[^025-10] anuyuñjetha ||  
mā kāmaratisanthavaṃ[^025-11] ||  
appamatto hi jhāyanto[^025-12] ||  
pappoti paramaṃ sukhan ti[^025-13] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 026 -->
<a id="page-026"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 026]*

SN_1.1,4.7. Samayo.

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ mahāvane mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhim pañcamattehi bhikkhusatehi sabbeh-eva arahantehi ||  
dasahi ca lokadhātūhi devatāyo[^026-1] yebhuyyena sannipatitā honti Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya bhikkhusaṅghañca || ||  

2. Atha kho catunnaṃ Suddhāvāsakāyikānaṃ devatānam[^026-2] etad ahosi ||  
Ayaṃ kho Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ mahāvane mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ pañcamattehi bhikkhusatehi sabbeh-eva arahantehi ||  
dasahi ca lokadhātūhi devatāyo yebhuyyena sannipatitā honti Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya bhikkhusaṅghañca ||  
Yannūna mayaṃ pi[^026-3] yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkameyyāma ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavato santike[^026-4] pacceka[^026-5] gāthaṃ bhāseyyāmāti || ||  

3. Atha kho tā devatāyo seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāham pasāreyya ||  
pasāritaṃ vā bāham sammiñjeyya[^026-6] ||  
evam evaṃ Suddhāvāsesu devesu antarahitā Bhagavato purato pātur ahesuṃ[^026-7] || ||  

4. Atha kho tā devatāyo Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Mahāsamayo pavanasmiṃ ||  
devakāyā samāgatā ||  
āgatamha imaṃ dhammasamayaṃ ||  
dakkhitāye aparājitasaṅghan ti || ||  

5. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Tatra bhikkhavo samādahaṃsu ||  
cittam attano ujukam akaṃsu ||  
sārathī va nettāni gahetvā ||  
indriyāni rakkhanti paṇḍitā ti. || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 027 -->
<a id="page-027"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 027]*

6. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike {imaṃ} gātham abhāsi || ||  
Chetvā[^027-1] khilam[^027-2] chetvā palighaṃ ||  
indakhīlam ohacca[^027-3]-m-anejā ||  
te caranti suddhā vimalā ||  
cakkhumatā[^027-4] sudantā susunāgā ti || ||  

7. Atha kho aparā devatā || pa ||  
Ye keci Buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatāse ||  
na te gamissanti apāyabhūmiṃ[^027-5] ||  
pahāya mānusaṃ dehaṃ ||  
devakāyaṃ paripuressantīti[^027-6] || ||  

SN_1.1,4.8. Sakalikam.

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Maddakucchismiṃ migadāye || ||  

2. Tena kho pana samayena Bhagavato pādo[^027-7] sakalikāya[^027-8] khato hoti ||  
Bhūsā sudaṃ Bhagavato vedanā[^027-9] vattanti sarīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā ||  
Tā sudaṃ Bhagavā sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno || ||  

3. Atha kho Bhagavā catugguṇam[^027-10] saṅghāṭiṃ paññāpetvā dakkhiṇena passena sīhaseyyam kappesi[^027-11] pāde pādam accādhāya sato sampajāno || ||  

4. Atha kho sattasatā Satullapakāyikā devatāyo abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Maddakucchim obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkamiṃsu || ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu || ||  

5. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho ekā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 028 -->
<a id="page-028"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 028]*

Nāgo vata bho samaṇo Gotamo ||  
nāgavatā ca samuppannā[^028-1] sārīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā ||  
sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno ti || ||  

6. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Sīho vata bho samaṇo Gotamo ||  
sīhavatā ca samuppannā sārīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā ||  
sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno ti || ||  

7. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Ājānīyo vata bho samaṇo Gotamo ||  
ājānīyavatā ca samuppannā sārīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā ||  
sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno ti || ||  

8. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Nisabho vata bho Samaṇo Gotamo ||  
nisabhavatā ca samuppannā sārīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā ||  
sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno ti || ||  

9. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Dhorayho vata bho samaṇo Gotamo ||  
dhorayhavatā ca samuppannā sārīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asāta amanāpā ||  
sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno ti || ||  

10. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi || ||  
Danto vata bho samaṇo Gotamo ||  
dantavatā ca samuppannā sārīrikā vedanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā ||  
sato sampajāno adhivāseti avihaññamāno ti || ||  

11. Atha kho aparā devatā Bhagavato santike imam udānam udānesi[^028-2] || ||  
Passa samādhi-subhāvitaṃ[^028-3] cittaṃ ca vimuttaṃ ||  
na cābhinatam[^028-4] na cāpanatam[^028-5] na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayha cāritavatam[^028-6] ||  
Yo evarūpam purisanāgam purisasīhaṃ purisa-ḥ


<!-- PTS Page 029 -->
<a id="page-029"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 029]*

ājānīyaṃ purisa-nisabhaṃ purisadhorayham purisadantam atikkamitabbaṃ maññeyya kim aññatra adassanā ti || ||  
Pañcavedasataṃ[^029-1] samaṃ ||  
tapassībrāhmaṇācaraṃ[^029-2] ||  
cittaṃ ca nesaṃ na sammā vimuttaṃ ||  
hīnattarūpā[^029-3] na pāraṃgamā te ||  
Taṇhādhipannā vata sīlabaddhā[^029-4] ||  
lūkhaṃ tapaṃ vassasataṃ carantā ||  
Cittaṃ ca nesaṃ na sammā vimuttaṃ ||  
hīnattarūpā na pāraṃgamā te[^029-5] || ||  
Na mānakāmassa damo idh-atthi ||  
na monam atthi asamāhitassa ||  
eko araññe viharaṃ pamatto ||  
na maccudheyyassa tareyya pāraṃ[^029-6] || ||  
Mānam pahāya susamāhitatto ||  
sucetaso sabbadhi vippamutto ||  
eko araññe viharaṃ appamatto ||  
sa maccudheyyassa tareyya pāran ti || ||  

SN_1.1,4.9. Pajjunna-dhītā (1).

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekam samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati mahāvane Kūṭāgāra-sālāyaṃ || ||  

2. Atha kho Kokanadā[^029-7] Pajjunnassa dhītā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ mahāvanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi[^029-8] ||  

3. Ekam antam ṭhitā kho sa devatā Kokanadā Pajjunnassa dhītā Bhagavato santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  
Vesāliyam vane viharantaṃ ||  
aggaṃ sattassa sambuddhaṃ ||  


<!-- PTS Page 030 -->
<a id="page-030"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 030]*

Kokanadā-h-asmiṃ abhivande ||  
Kokanadā Pajjunnassa dhītā[^030-1] || ||  
Sutam eva me pure[^030-2] āsi dhammo ||  
cakkhumatānubuddho ||  
sā-ham dāni[^030-3] sakkhi jānāmi ||  
munino desayato Sugatassa ||  
Ye hi keci ariyadhammaṃ[^030-4] ||  
vigarahantā[^030-5] caranti dummedhā ||  
upenti Roruvaṃ ghoraṃ ||  
cirarattaṃ dukkham anubhavanti || ||  
Ye ca kho ariyadhamme[^030-6] ||  
khantiyā upasamena upetā ||  
pahāya mānusaṃ dehaṃ ||  
devakāyam paripuressantī ti[^030-7] || ||  

SN_1.1,4.10. Pajjunna-dhitā (2).

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekam samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati magāvane Kūṭāgāra-sālāyaṃ || ||  

2. Atha kho Cūḷa-Kokanadā[^030-8] Pajjunnassa dhītā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ mahāvanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Cūḷa-Kokanadā Pajjunnassa dhītā Bhagavato santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  
Idhāgamā[^030-9] vijjupabhāsavaṇṇā ||  
Kokanadā Pajjunnassa dhītā ||  
buddhaṃ ca dhammaṃ ca namassamānā ||  
gāthā c-imā atthavatī abhāsi || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 031 -->
<a id="page-031"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 031]*

Bahunā pi kho taṃ[^031-1] vibhajeyyaṃ ||  
pariyāyena tādiso dhammo ||  
saṅkhittam atthaṃ lapayissāmi ||  
yāvatā me manasā pariyattaṃ || ||  
Pāpaṃ na kayirā[^031-2] vacasā manasā[^031-3] ||  
kāyena vā[^031-4] kiñcana sabbaloke ||  
kāme pahāya satimā sampajaño ||  
dukkhaṃ na sevetha anatthasaṃhitan ti[^031-5] || ||  
Satullapakāyika-vaggo catuttho || ||  
Tass-uddānam || ||  
Sabbhi Maccharinā Sādhu ||  
Na sant-Ujjhānasaññino ||  
Saddhā Samayo Sakalikaṃ ||  
ubho Pajjunna-dhītaro ti || ||  

___________________________________________

CHAPTER V. ĀDITTA-VAGGO. Evam me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārame || ||  
Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antam ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavato santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  

SN_1.1,5.1. Ādittam. Ādittasmim agārasmiṃ ||  
yaṃ nīharati bhājanaṃ ||  
taṃ tassa hoti atthāya ||  
no ca yaṃ tattha ḍayhatīti[^031-6] || ||  
Evam ādīpito[^031-7] loko ||  
jarāya maraṇena ca ||  
nīhareth-eva dānena ||  
dinnaṃ hoti sunīhataṃ[^031-8] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 032 -->
<a id="page-032"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 032]*

dinnaṃ sukhaphalaṃ[^032-1] hoti ||  
nādinnam hoti taṃ tathā ||  
corā haranti rājāno ||  
aggī[^032-2] ḍayhati nassati || ||  
Atha antena jahāti ||  
sarīraṃ sapariggahaṃ ||  
etad aññāya medhāvi ||  
bhuñjetha ca[^032-3] dadetha ca ||  
datvā[^032-4] bhutvā ca yathānubhāvaṃ ||  
anindito saggam upeti ṭhānan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.2. Kiṃdada. Kiṃdado balado hoti ||  
kiṃdado hoti vaṇṇado ||  
kiṃdado sukhado hoti ||  
kiṃdado hoti cakkhudo ||  
ko[^032-5] ca sabbadado hoti ||  
taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito || ||  
Annado balado hoti ||  
vatthado hoti vaṇṇado ||  
yānado sukhado hoti ||  
dīpado hoti cakkhudo ||  
so[^032-6] ca sabbadado hoti ||  
yo dadāti upassayaṃ ||  
amataṃ dado ca so hoti ||  
yo dhammam anusāsatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.3. Annam. Annam evābhinandanti ||  
ubhayo[^032-7] devamānusā ||  
atha ko[^032-8] nāma so yakkho ||  
yam annaṃ nābhinandatīti[^032-9] || ||  
Ye naṃ dadanti saddhāya ||  
vippasannena cetasā ||  
tam eva annaṃ bhajati ||  
asmiṃ loke paramhi ca || ||  
Tasmā vineyya maccheraṃ ||  
dajjā dānaṃ malābhibhū ||  
puññāni paralokasmiṃ ||  
patiṭṭhā honti pāṇinan ti[^032-10] || ||  

SN_1.1,5.4. Ekamūla. Ekamūlaṃ dvirāvaṭṭam[^032-11] ||  
timalaṃ pañcapattharaṃ ||  
samuddaṃ dvādasāvaṭṭaṃ[^032-12] ||  
pātālam atarī[^032-13] isīti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 033 -->
<a id="page-033"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 033]*

SN_1.1,5.5. Anomiya. Anomanāmaṃ nipuṇatthadassiṃ[^033-1] ||  
paññādadaṃ kāmalaye asattaṃ ||  
taṃ passatha sabbaviduṃ sumedhaṃ ||  
ariye pathe kamamānaṃ mahesin ti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.6. Accharā. Accharāgaṇasaṅghuṭṭhaṃ ||  
pisācagaṇasevitaṃ ||  
vanan-tam mohanaṃ nāma ||  
kathaṃ yātrā bhavissatīti || ||  
Ujuko nāma so maggo ||  
abhayā nāma sā disā ||  
ratho akujano[^033-2] nāma ||  
dhammacakkehi saṃyuto[^033-3] || ||  
Hirī tassa apālambo ||  
saty-assa[^033-4] parivāraṇam ||  
dhammāhaṃ sārathiṃ[^033-5] brūmi ||  
sammādiṭṭhipure javaṃ || ||  
Yassa etādisaṃ[^033-6] yānaṃ ||  
itthiyā purisassa vā ||  
sa ve[^033-7] etena yānena ||  
nibbānass-eva santike ti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.7. Vanaropa (or Vacanam). Kesaṃ divā ca ratto ca ||  
sadā puññaṃ pavaḍḍhati ||  
dhammaṭṭhā sīlasampannā ||  
ke janā saggagāmino ti || ||  
Arāmaropā vanaropā ||  
ye janā setukārakā ||  
papañ ca udapānañ ca ||ye dadanti upassayaṃ[^033-8] ||  
tesaṃ divā ca ratto ca ||  
sadā puññaṃ pavaḍḍhati ||  
dhammaṭṭhā sīlasampannā ||  
te janā saggagāmino ti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.8. Jetavana. Idaṃ hitaṃ Jetavanaṃ ||  
isisaṅghanisevitaṃ ||  
āvutthaṃ[^033-9] dhammarājena ||  
pītisañjananam mama || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 034 -->
<a id="page-034"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 034]*

kammaṃ vijjā ca dhammo ca ||  
sīlaṃ jīvitam uttamaṃ ||  
etena maccā sujjhanti ||  
na gottena dhanena vā || ||  
Tasmā hi paṇḍito poso ||  
sampassaṃ attham attano ||  
yoniso vicine dhammaṃ ||  
evaṃ tattha visujjhati || ||  
Sāriputto va paññāya ||  
sīlena upasamena ca ||  
yo pi pāragato[^034-1] bhikkhu ||  
etāva paramo siyāti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.9. Macchari. Ye dha[^034-2] maccharino loke ||  
kadariyā paribhāsakā ||  
aññesaṃ dadamānānaṃ ||  
antarāyakarā narā || ||  
kiṃdiso[^034-3] tesaṃ vipāko ||  
samparāyo ca kiṃdiso ||  
bhavantaṃ[^034-4] puṭṭhum āgamma ||  
kathaṃ jānemu tam mayan ti || ||  
Ye dha maccharino loke ||  
kadariyā paribhāsakā ||  
aññesaṃ dadamānānam ||  
antarāyakarā narā || ||  
nirayaṃ tiracchānayoniṃ ||  
yamalokam uppajjare ||  
sace enti manussattaṃ ||  
dalidde jāyare kule || ||  
coḷaṃ piṇḍo ratī khiḍḍā ||  
yattha kicchena[^034-5] labbhati ||  
parato āsiṃsare bālā ||  
tam pi tesaṃ na labbhati ||  
diṭṭhe dhamme sa vipāko ||  
samparāye[^034-6] ca duggatīti || ||  
Iti h-etam vijānāma ||  
aññaṃ pucchāma Gotama[^034-7] ||  
ye dha[^034-8] laddhā manussattaṃ ||  
vadaññū vītamaccharā ||  
buddhe pasannā dhamme ca ||  
saṅghe ca tibbagāravā || ||  
kiṃdiso[^034-9] tesaṃ vipāko ||  
samparāyo ca kiṃdiso ||  
bhavantaṃ[^034-10] puṭṭhum āgamma ||  
kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayan ti || ||  
Ye dha laddhā manussattam ||  
vadaññū vītamaccharā ||  
buddhe pasannā dhamme ca ||  
saṅghe tibbagāravā ||  
ete sagge pakāsenti[^034-11] ||  
yattha te upapajjare[^034-12] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 035 -->
<a id="page-035"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 035]*

sace enti manussattaṃ ||  
aḍḍhe ajāyare kule ||  
coḷaṃ piṇḍo ratī khiḍḍā ||  
yatthākicchena[^035-1] labbhati || ||  
parasambhatesu bhogesu ||  
vasavattīva modare[^035-2] ||  
diṭṭhe dhamme sa vipāko ||  
samparāye[^035-3] ca suggatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,5.10. Ghaṭīkaro. Avihaṃ upapannāse ||  
vimuttā satta bhikkhavo ||  
rāgadosaparikkhīṇā ||  
tiṇṇā loke visattikan ti || ||  
Ke ca te[^035-4] ataruṃ paṅkaṃ[^035-5] ||  
maccudheyyaṃ suduttaraṃ ||  
te[^035-6] hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ ||  
dibbayogam[^035-7] upaccagun ti || ||  
Upako Phalagaṇḍo ca[^035-8] ||  
Pukkusāti ca te tayo ||  
Bhaddiyo Khaṇḍadevo ca[^035-9] ||  
Bāhuraggi[^035-10] ca Piṅgiyo[^035-11] ||  
te hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ ||  
dibbayogam upaccagun ti || ||  
Kusalaṃ[^035-12] bhāsasi[^035-13] tesaṃ ||  
mārapāsappahāyinaṃ ||  
kassa te dhammam aññāya ||  
acchiduṃ[^035-14] bhavabhandhanan ti || ||  
Na aññatra Bhagavatā ||  
naññatra[^035-15] tava sāsanā ||  
yassa te dhammam aññāya ||  
acchiduṃ bhavabandhanaṃ || ||  
yattha nāmañca rupañca ||  
asesam uparujjhati ||  
taṃ te dhammam idha ñāya ||  
acchiduṃ bhavabandhanan ti || ||  
Gambhīraṃ bhāsasi[^035-16] vācaṃ ||  
dubbijānam sudubbudhaṃ ||  
kassa tvaṃ dhammam aññaya[^035-17] ||  
vācam[^035-18] bhāsasi īdisanti || ||  
Kumbhakāro pure āsiṃ ||  
Vehaḷiṅge[^035-19] ghaṭīkaro ||  
mātāpettibharo āsiṃ ||  
Kassapassa upāsako[^035-20] ||

##### *[PTS Page 036]*

virato methunā dhammā ||  
brahmacārī nirāmiso ||  
ahuvā te sagāmeyyo ||  
ahuvā te pure sakhā ||  
so-ham ete pajānāmi ||  
vimutte[^036-1] satta bhikkhave[^036-2] ||  
rāgadosaparikkhīṇe ||  
tiṇṇe[^036-3] loke visattikan ti || ||  
Evam etaṃ tadā āsi ||  
yathā bhāsasi Bhaggava ||  
kumbhakāro pure āsi ||  
Vehaḷiṅge ghaṭikāro ||

virato methunā dhammā ||  
brahmacārī nirāmiso ||  
ahuvā me sagāmeyyo ||  
ahuvā me pure sakhā ti || ||  
evam etaṃ purāṇānaṃ ||  
sahāyānam ahu saṅgamo ||  
ubhinnaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ ||  
sarīrantimadhārinan ti[^036-4] || ||  
Āditta-vaggo pañcamo || ||  
Tass-uddānaṃ ||

CHAPTER VI. JARĀ-VAGGO.

SN_1.1,6.1. Jarā. Kiṃsu yāva jarā sādhu ||  
kiṃsu sādhu patiṭṭhitaṃ[^036-6] ||  
kiṃsu narānaṃ ratanaṃ ||  
kiṃsu corehi duharan ti || ||  
Sīlaṃ yāva jarā sādhu ||  
saddhā sādhu patiṭṭhitā[^036-7] ||  
paññā narānaṃ ratanaṃ ||  
puññaṃ corehi duharan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.2. Ajarasā. Kiṃsu ajarasā sādhu ||  
kiṃsu sādhu adhiṭṭhitaṃ ||  
kiṃsu narānaṃ ratanaṃ ||  
kiṃsu corehi ahāriyan ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 037 -->
<a id="page-037"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 037]*

Sīlam ajarasā sādhu ||  
saddhā sādhu adhiṭṭhitā[^037-1] ||  
paññā narānaṃ ratanaṃ ||  
puññaṃ corehi hāriyan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.3. Mittam. Kiṃsu pathavato[^037-2] mittaṃ ||  
kiṃsu mittaṃ sake ghare ||  
kiṃ mittaṃ[^037-3] atthajātassa ||  
kiṃ mittam samparāyikanti || ||  
Sattho[^037-4] pathavato[^037-5] mittam ||  
mātā mittaṃ sake ghare ||  
sahāyo atthajātassa ||  
hoti mittaṃ punappunaṃ ||  
sayam katāni puññāni ||  
taṃ mittaṃ samparāyikan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.4. Vatthu. Kiṃsu vatthu manussānaṃ ||  
kiṃsu-dha paramā sakhā ||  
kiṃsu bhūtā upajīvanti ||  
ye pāṇā pathaviṃ sitā ti[^037-6] || ||  
Puttā vatthu[^037-7] manussānaṃ ||  
bhariyā ca paramā sakhā ||  
vuṭṭhibhūtā[^037-8] upajīvanti ||  
ye pāṇā pathaviṃ sitā ti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.5. Janaṃ (1). Kiṃsu janeti purisaṃ ||  
kiṃsu[^037-9] tassa vidhāvati ||  
kiṃsu saṃsāram āpādi[^037-10] ||  
kiṃsu tassa mahabbhayan ti || ||  
Taṇhā janeti purisaṃ ||  
cittam assa vidhāvati ||  
satto saṃsāram āpādi ||  
dukkham assa mahabbhayan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.6. Janaṃ (2). Kiṃsu janeti purisaṃ ||  
kiṃsu tassa vidhāvati ||  
kiṃsu saṃsāram āpādi ||  
kismā[^037-11] na parimuccatīti || ||  
Taṇhā janeti purisaṃ ||  
cittam assa vidhāvati ||  
satto saṃsāram āpādi ||  
dukkhā na parimuccatī ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 038 -->
<a id="page-038"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 038]*

SN_1.1,6.7. Janaṃ (3). Kiṃsu janeti purisaṃ ||  
kiṃsu tassa vidhāvati ||  
kiṃsu saṃsāram āpādi ||  
kiṃsu tassa parāyanan ti || ||  
Taṇhā janeti purisaṃ ||  
cittam assa vidhāvati ||  
satto saṃsāram āpādi ||  
kammaṃ tassa parāyanan ti[^038-1] || ||  

SN_1.1,6.8. Uppatho.2 Kiṃsu uppatho akkhāti ||  
kiṃsu rattindivakkhayo ||  
kiṃ malaṃ[^038-3] brahmacariyassa ||  
kim sinānam anodakanti || ||  
Rāgo uppatho akkhāti[^038-4] ||  
vayo rattindivakkhayo ||  
itthi malaṃ brahmacariyassa ||  
etthāyaṃ sajjate[^038-5] pajā ||  
tapo brahmacariyañca ||  
taṃ sinānam anodakan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.9. Dutiyo. Kiṃsu dutiyaṃ purisassa hoti ||  
kiṃsu c-enaṃ pasāsati ||  
kissa cābhirato[^038-6] macco ||  
sabbadukkhā pamuccatīti || ||  
Saddhā dutiyā purisassa hoti ||  
paññā c-enaṃ pasāsati ||  
nibbānābhirato macco ||  
sabbadukkhā pamuccatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,6.10. Kavi. Kiṃsu nidānaṃ gāthānaṃ[^038-7] ||  
kiṃsu tāsaṃ viyañjanaṃ[^038-7] ||  
kiṃsu sannissitā gāthā ||  
kiṃsu gāthānam āsayo ti || ||  
Chando nidānaṃ gāthānaṃ ||  
akkharā tāsaṃ viyañjanaṃ[^038-8] ||  
nāmasannissitā[^038-9] gāthā ||  
kavi[^038-10] gāthānam āsayo ti || ||  
Jarā-vaggo chaṭṭho ||  
Tass-uddānam || ||  
Jarā Ajarasā Mittaṃ ||  
Vatthu tīṇi Janāni ca || Uppatho ca Dutiyo ca ||  
Kavinā purito vaggo ti || ||

<a id="page-039"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 039]*

CHAPTER VII. ADDHA-VAGGO.

SN_1.1,7.1. NĀMAM. Kiṃsu sabbaṃ addhabhavi[^039-1] ||  
kismā[^039-2] bhīyo na vijjati ||  
kissassa ekadhammassa ||  
sabbeva vasam anvagū ti || ||  
Nāmam sabbam addhabhavi ||  
nāmā bhīyo na vijjati ||  
nāmassa ekadhammassa ||  
sabbeva vasam anvagū ti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.2. Cittam. Kenassu nīyati[^039-3] loko ||  
kenassu parikissati[^039-4] ||  
kissassa ekadhammassa ||  
sabbeva vasam anvagūti || ||  
Cittena nīyati[^039-5] loko ||  
cittena parikissati ||  
cittassa ekadhammassa ||  
sabbeva vasam anvagūti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.3. Taṇhā. Kenassu nīyati loko ||  
kenassu parikissati ||  
kissassa ekadhammassa ||  
sabbeva vasam anvagū ti || ||  
Taṇhāya nīyati loko ||  
taṇhāya parikissati ||  
taṇhāya ekadhammassa ||  
sabbeva vasam anvagū ti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.4. Samyojana. Kiṃsu saṃyojano loko ||  
kiṃsu tassa vicāraṇam ||  
kissassa[^039-6] vippahānena ||  
nibbānam iti vuccatīti || ||  
Nandī saṃyojano loko ||  
vitakk-assa vicāraṇam[^039-7] ||  
taṇhāya vippahānena ||  
nibbānam iti vuccatīti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.5. Bandhana. Kiṃsu sambandhano loko ||  
kiṃsu tassa vicāranaṃ || ||  
kissassa vippahānena ||  
sabbaṃ chindati bandhanan ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 040 -->
<a id="page-040"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 040]*

Nandī sambandhano loko ||  
vitakk-assa vicāranaṃ ||  
taṇhāya vippahānena ||  
sabbaṃ chindati bandhanan ti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.6. Abbhāhata. Kenassu-bbhāhato[^040-1] loko ||  
kenassu[^040-2] parivārito ||  
kena sallena otiṇṇo ||  
kissa dhūpāyito[^040-3] sadā ti || ||  
Maccunābbhāhato loko ||  
jarāya parivārito ||  
taṇhāsallena otiṇṇo ||  
icchādhūpāyito[^040-4] sadā ti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.7. Uḍḍito. Kenassu uḍḍito[^040-5] loko ||  
kenassu parivārito ||  
kenassu[^040-6] pihito loko ||  
kismiṃ loko patiṭṭhito ti || ||  
Taṇhāya uḍḍito loko ||  
jarāya parivārito ||  
maccunā pihito loko ||  
dukkhe loko patiṭṭhito ti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.8. Pihito. Kenassu pihito loko ||  
kosmiṃ loko patiṭṭhito || ||  
kenassu uḍḍito[^040-7] loko ||  
kenassu parivārito ti || ||  
Maccunā pihito loko ||  
dukkhe loko patiṭṭhito ||  
taṇhāya uḍḍito loko ||  
jarāya parivārito ti || ||  

SN_1.1,7.9. Icchā. Kenassu bajjhati[^040-8] loko ||  
kissa vinayāya muccati ||  
kissassu[^040-9] vippahānena ||  
sabbaṃ chindati bandhananti || ||  
Icchāya bajjhati loko ||  
icchāvinayāya muccati ||  
icchāya vippahānena ||  
sabbaṃ chindati bandhanan ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 041 -->
<a id="page-041"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 041]*

SN_1.1,7.10. Loka. Kismiṃ loko samuppanno ||  
kismiṃ kubbati santhavaṃ ||  
kissā[^041-1] loko upādāya ||  
kismiṃ loko vihaññatīti || ||  
Chasu[^041-2] loko samuppanno ||  
chasu kubbati santhavaṃ ||  
channam eva upādāya ||  
chasu loko vihaññatīti || ||  
Addha-vaggo sattamo || ||  
Tass-uddānam || ||  
Nāmam Cittaṃ ca Taṇhā ca ||  
Saṃyojanaṃ ca Bandhanā ||  
Abbhāhat-Uḍḍito[^041-3] Pihito ||  
Icchā Lokena te dasā ti || ||  

___________________________________________

CHAPTER VIII. CHETVĀ-VAGGO. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  

SN_1.1,8.1. Chetvā. Kiṃsu chetvā[^041-4] sukhaṃ seti ||  
kiṃsu chetvā na socati ||  
kissassa[^041-5] ekadhammassa ||  
vadhaṃ rocesi Gotamā ti || ||  
Kodhaṃ chetvā sukhaṃ seti ||  
kodhaṃ chetvā na socāti ||  
kodhassa vīsamūlassa ||  
madhuraggassa devate ||  
vadham ariyā pasaṃsanti ||  
taṃ hi chetvā na socatīti[^041-6] || ||  

SN_1.1,8.2. Ratha. Kiṃsu rathassa paññāṇaṃ ||  
kiṃsu paññāṇam aggino ||  
kiṃsu raṭṭhassa paññāṇaṃ ||  
kiṃsu paññāṇam itthiyā ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 042 -->
<a id="page-042"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 042]*

Dhajo rathassa paññāṇam ||  
dhūmo paññāṇam aggino ||  
rājā raṭṭhassa paññāṇam ||  
bhattā paññāṇam itthiyāti || ||  

SN_1.1,8.3. Vitta. Kiṃsūdha vittaṃ purisassa seṭṭhaṃ ||  
kiṃsu suciṇṇo sukham āvahāti ||  
kiṃsu have sādutaraṃ[^042-1] rasānaṃ ||  
kathaṃ[^042-2] jīviṃ jūvitam āhu seṭṭhan ti || ||  
Saddhīdha vittaṃ purisassa seṭṭham ||  
dhammo suciṇṇo sukham āvahāti ||  
saccam have sādutaram rasānam ||  
paññājīviṃ jīvitaṃ āhu setthaṇ ti[^042-3] || ||  

SN_1.1,8.4. Vuṭṭhi. Kiṃsu uppatataṃ seṭṭham ||  
kiṃsu nipatataṃ varaṃ ||  
kiṃsu pavajamānānaṃ ||  
kiṃsu pavadataṃ varan ti || ||  
Bījam[^042-4] uppatataṃ seṭṭham ||  
vuṭṭhi nipatatam varā[^042-5] ||  
gāvo pavajamānānam ||  
putto pavadataṃ varo ti || ||  
Vijjā uppatataṃ seṭṭhā[^042-6] ||  
avijjā nipatataṃ varā ||  
saṅgho pavajamānānaṃ ||  
buddho pavadataṃ varo ti || ||  

SN_1.1,8.5. Bhītā. Kiṃsūdha bhīrā[^042-7] janatā anekā ||  
maggo v-anekāyatanaṃ pavutto ||  
pucchāmi taṃ Gotama bhūripañña ||  
kismiṃ ṭhito paralokaṃ na bhāye ti || ||  
Vācaṃ manañca paṇidhāya sammā ||  
kāyena pāpāni akubbamāno ||  
bahvannapānaṃ[^042-8] gharam āvasanto ||  


<!-- PTS Page 043 -->
<a id="page-043"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 043]*

saddho mudū {saṃvibhāgī} vadaññū ||  
etesu dhammesu ṭhito catusu ||  
paralokaṃ na bhāye ti || ||  

SN_1.1,8.6. Na jīrati. Kiṃ[^043-1] jīrati kiṃ na jīrati ||  
kiṃ[^043-1] uppatho ti vuccati ||  
kiṃsu dhammānaṃ paripantho[^043-2] ||  
kiṃsu rattindivakkhayo[^043-3] ||  
kiṃ malaṃ brahmacariyassa ||  
kiṃ sinānam anodakaṃ ||  
kati lokasmiṃ chiddāni ||  
yattha cittaṃ na tiṭṭhati[^043-4] ||  
bhavantaṃ[^043-5] puṭṭhum āgamma ||  
katham jānemu tam mayan ti || ||  
Rūpaṃ jīrati maccānam ||  
nāmagottaṃ na jīrati ||  
rāgo uppatho ti vuccati ||  
lobho dhammānaṃ paripantho[^043-6] ||  
vayo rattindivakkhayo ||  
itthi malam brahmacariyassa ||  
etthāyam sajjate[^043-7] pajā ||  
tapo ca brahmacariyañca ||  
tam sinānam anodakaṃ[^043-8] || ||  
Cha lokasmiṃ chiddāni[^043-9] yattha cittaṃ na tiṭṭhati ||  
alassañca[^043-10] pamādoca ||  
anuṭṭhānaṃ asaṃyamo ||  
niddā tandī ca te chidde ||  
sabbaso taṃ vivajjaye ti || ||  

SN_1.1,8.7. Issaraṃ. Kiṃsu issariyaṃ loke ||  
kiṃsu bhaṇḍānam uttamaṃ ||  
kiṃsu satthamalaṃ[^043-11] loke ||  
kiṃsu lokasmim abbudaṃ || ||  
kiṃsu harantaṃ[^043-12] vārenti ||  
haranto[^043-12] pana[^043-13] ko piyo ||  
kiṃsu punappanāyantaṃ ||  
abhinandanti paṇḍitāti || ||  
Vaso[^043-14] issariyaṃ loke ||  
itthi bhaṇḍānam[^043-15] uttamaṃ ||  
kodho satthamalam loke ||  
corā lokasmim abbudā || ||  
coram harantaṃ[^043-16] vārenti ||  
haranto[^043-17] samaṇo piyo ||  
samaṇam punappunāyantaṃ ||  
abhinandanti paṇḍitāti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 044 -->
<a id="page-044"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 044]*

SN_1.1,8.8. Kāma. Kiṃ atthakāmo na dade ||  
kiṃ macco na pariccaje ||  
kiṃsu mucceyya[^044-1] kalyāṇam[^044-2] ||  
pāpiyaṃ[^044-3] ca na mocayeti || ||  
Attānaṃ na dade poso ||  
attānam na pariccaje ||  
vācaṃ muñceyya kalyāṇiṃ[^044-4] ||  
pāpikaṃ[^044-5] ca na mocaye ti || ||  

SN_1.1,8.9. Pātheyyaṃ. Kiṃsu bandhati pātheyyaṃ ||  
kiṃsu bhogānam āsayo ||  
kiṃsu naraṃ parikassati[^044-6] ||  
kiṃsu lokasmiṃ dujjahaṃ ||  
kismiṃ baddhā[^044-7] puthusattā ||  
pāsena sakunī yathā ti || ||  
Saddhā bandhati pātheyyam ||  
siri[^044-8] bhogānam āsayo ||  
icchā naram parikassati ||  
icchā lokasmiṃ dujjahā ||  
icchābaddhā[^044-9] puthusattā ||  
pāsena sakuṇī yathā ti || ||  

SN_1.1,8.10. Pajjoto. Kiṃsu lokasmiṃ pajjoto ||  
kiṃsu lokasmiṃ jāgaro ||  
kiṃsu kamme sajīvānaṃ ||  
kim assa[^044-10] iriyāpatho. || ||  
kiṃsu alasam analasañca[^044-11] ||  
mātā puttaṃ va posati ||  
kiṃsu bhūtā upajīvanti ||  
ye pāṇā pathaviṃ sītāti[^044-12] || ||  
Paññā lokasmiṃ pajjoto ||  
sati lokasmiṃ jāgaro ||  
gāvo kamme sajīvānaṃ ||  
sītassa[^044-13] iriyāpatho ||  
vuṭṭhi alasam analasañca[^044-14] ||  
mātā puttaṃ va posati ||  
vuṭṭhibhūtā upajīvanti ||  
ye pāṇā pathaviṃ sitā ti[^044-15] || ||  

SN_1.1,8.11. Araṇā. Kesu-dha araṇā loke ||  
kesaṃ vusiṭaṃ na nassati ||  
ke-dha icchaṃ[^044-16] parijānanti ||  
kesaṃ bhojisiyaṃ sadā || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 045 -->
<a id="page-045"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 045]*

kiṃsu mātā pitā bhātā ||  
vandanti naṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ ||  
kaṃsu idha[^045-1] jātihīnaṃ ||  
abhivādenti khattiyā ti || ||  
Samaṇīdha araṇā loke ||  
samaṇānaṃ vusitaṃ na nassati ||  
samaṇā icchaṃ[^045-2] parijānanti ||  
samaṇānaṃ bhojisiyaṃ sadā || ||  
Samaṇaṃ mātā pitā bhātā ||  
vandanti naṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ ||  
samaṇīdha[^045-3] jātihīnaṃ ||  
abhivādenti khattiyā ti || ||  
Chetvā-vaggo aṭṭhamo ||  
Tass-uddānam Chetvā Rathaṃ ca Vittaṃ ca ||  
Vuṭṭhi Bhītā Na-jīrati ||  
Issaraṃ Kāmaṃ Pātheyyaṃ ||  
Pajjoto Araṇena cā ti || ||  
Devatā-Samyuttaṃ samattaṃ || ||

##### *[PTS Page 046]*

********************************************

BOOK II.- DEVAPUTTA-SAṂYUTTAM.

___________________________________________

CHAPTER I. PATHAMO-VAGGO.

SN_1.2,1.1. Kassapo (1).

1. Evam me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||  
Atha kho Kassapo devaputto abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappam Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antam ṭhito[^046-1] kho Kassapo devaputto Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Bhikkhuṃ Bhagavā pakāsesi no ca bhikkhuno anusāsan ti[^046-2] || ||  

2. Tena hi Kassapa taññev-etthā[^046-3] paṭibhātūti || ||  

3. Subhāsitassa sikkhetha[^046-4] ||  
samaṇupāsanassa ca ||  
ekāsanassa ca raho ||  
cittavūpāsamassa cā ti || ||  

4. Idam avoca Kassapo devaputto ||  
samanuñño satthā ahosi || ||  
Atha kho Kassapo devaputto samanuñño me satthāti Bhagavantaṃ vanditvā[^046-5] padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthev-antaradhāyīti || ||  

SN_1.2,1.2. Kassapo (2).

1. Sāvatthiyaṃ ārāme || ||  

2. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Kassapo devaputto Bhagavato santike {imaṃ} gātham abhāsi || ||  
Bhikkhu siyā jhāyī[^046-6] vimuttacitto ||  
ākaṅkhe ca[^046-7] hadayassānuppattiṃ ||  
lokassa ñatvā udayabbayañca[^046-8] ||  
sucetaso asito[^046-9] tadānisaṃso ti[^046-10] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 047 -->
<a id="page-047"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 047]*

SN_1.2,1.3. Māgho.

1. Sāvatthiyam ārāme || ||  

2. Atha kho Māgho devaputto abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho[^047-1] Māgho devaputto Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  

3. Kiṃsu chetvā[^047-2] sukhaṃ seti ||  
kiṃsu chetvā[^047-2] na socati ||  
kissassa ekadhammassa ||  
vadhaṃ rocesi Gotamāti || ||  

4. Kodhaṃ chetvā sukhaṃ seti ||  
kodhaṃ chetvā na socati ||  
kodhassa visamūlassa ||  
madhuraggassa Vatrabhū ||  
vadham ariyā pasaṃsanti ||  
taṃ hi chetvā na socatīti[^047-3] || ||  

SN_1.2,1.4. Māgadho.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Māgadho[^047-4] devaputto Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Kati lokasmiṃ pajjotā ||  
yehi loko pakāsati ||  
bhavantaṃ[^047-5] puṭṭhum āgamma ||  
kathaṃ janemu taṃ mayan ti || ||  

2. Cattāro loke pajjotā[^047-6] ||  
pañcam-ettha na vajjati ||  
divā tapati ādicco ||  
rattim ābhāti candimā ||  
atha aggi divārattiṃ ||  
tattha tattha pakāsati ||  
sambuddho tapataṃ seṭṭho ||  
esā ābhā anuttarā ti[^047-7] || ||  

SN_1.2,1.5. Dāmali.

1. Sāvatthiyam ārāme || ||  

2. Atha kho Dāmali[^047-8] devaputto abhikkantāyā rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Dāmali devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  

3. Karaṇīyam ettha[^047-9] brāhmaṇena ||  
padhānam akilāsunā ||  
kāmānaṃ vippahānena ||  
na tenāsiṃsate bhavan ti || ||  

4. Natthi kiccaṃ brāhmaṇassa[^047-10] ||  
Dāmalīti[^047-11] Bhagavā ||  
katakicco hi[^047-12] brāhmaṇo || ||  
Yāva na gādham labhati ||  


<!-- PTS Page 048 -->
<a id="page-048"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 048]*

nadīsu[^048-1] āyūhati sabbagattehi jantu ||  
gādhaṃ ca laddhāna thale ṭhito so ||  
nāyūhati pāragato hi so ti[^048-2] || ||  
es-upamā[^048-3] Dāmali brāhmaṇassa ||  
khīṇāsavassa nipakassa jhāyino ||  
pappuyya jāti-maraṇassa antaṃ ||  
nāyūhati pāragato hi so ti[^048-4] || ||  

SN_1.2,1.6. Kāmado.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Kāmado devaputto Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  

2. Dukkaram Bhagavā sudukkaraṃ Bhagavāti || ||  
Dukkaram vā pi karonti ||  
(Kāmadā ti Bhagavā) ||  
sekhasīlasamāhitā ṭhitattā ||  
anagāriyupetassa ||  
tuṭṭhi hoti sukhāvahā ti || ||  

3. Dullabhā Bhagavā yadidaṃ tuṭṭhī ti || ||  
Dullabhaṃ vā pi labhanti ||  
(Kāmadāti Bhagavā) cittavūpasame ratā ||  
yesaṃ divā ca ratto ca[^048-5] ||  
bhāvanāya rato mano ti || ||  

4. Dussamādahaṃ Bhagavā yad idaṃ cittan ti || ||  
Dussamādahaṃ vāpi samādahanti ||  
(Kāmadāti Bhagavā) ||  
indriyūpasame ratā ||  
te chetvā maccuno jālaṃ ||  
ariyā gacchanti Kāmadāti || ||  

5. Duggamo Bhagavā visamo maggo ti || ||  
Duggame visame vā pi ||  
ariyā gacchanti Kāmada[^048-6] ||  
anariyā visame magge ||  
papatanti avaṃsirā ||  
ariyānaṃ samo maggo ||  
ariyā hi visame samā ti || ||  

SN_1.2,1.7. Pañcālacaṇḍo.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Pañcālacaṇḍo devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Sambādhe vata okāsaṃ ||  
avindi bhūrimedhaso ||  
yo jhānam abuddhi buddho[^048-7] ||  
patilīnanisabho munīti || ||  

2. Sambādhe vāpi vindanti[^048-8] ||  
(Pañcālacaṇḍāti Bhagavā) ||  
dhammaṃ[^048-9] nibbānapattiyā ||  
ye satiṃ paccalatthaṃsu[^048-10] ||  
sammā[^048-11] te susamāhitā ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 049 -->
<a id="page-049"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 049]*

SN_1.2,1.8. Tāyano.

1. Atha kho Tāyano[^049-1] devaputto purāṇatitthakaro abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Tāyano devaputto Bhagavato santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  

2. Chinda sotaṃ parakkamma ||  
kāme panuda brāhmaṇa ||  
nappahāya muni[^049-2] kāme ||  
n-ekattam[^049-3] upapajjatīti || ||  
Kayirañ ce kayirath'; -enaṃ[^049-4] ||  
daḷham enaṃ parakkame ||  
sithilo[^049-5] hi paribbājo ||  
bhiyyo ākirate rajaṃ || ||  
Akatam dukkataṃ seyyo ||  
pacchā tapati dukkataṃ ||  
kataṃ ca sukataṃ[^049-6] seyyo ||  
yaṃ katvā nānutappati || ||  
Kuso yathā duggahīto ||  
hattham evānukantati ||  
sāmaññaṃ dupparāmaṭṭham[^049-7] ||  
nirayāyūpakaḍḍhati[^049-8] || ||  
Yaṃ kiñci sithilaṃ[^049-9] kammaṃ ||  
saṅkiliṭṭham ca yaṃ vataṃ ||  
saṅkassaraṃ brahmacariyaṃ ||  
na taṃ hoti mahapphalan ti[^049-10] || ||  

3. Idam avoca Tāyano devaputto ||  
idaṃ vatvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth-ev-antaradhāyīti || ||  

4. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi || ||  

5. Imaṃ bhikkhavo rattiṃ Tāyano nāma devaputto purāṇatitthakaro abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo[^049-11] kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā mam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Tāyano devaputto mama santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  

6. Chinda sotam parakkamma ||  
kāme panuda brāhmaṇa ||  
nappahāya muni kāme ||  
n-ekattam upapajjati ||  
Kayirañce kayirath-enaṃ ||  
daḷham enaṃ parakkame ||  


<!-- PTS Page 050 -->
<a id="page-050"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 050]*

sithilo hi paribbājo ||  
bhiyyo ākirate rajaṃ ||  
Akataṃ dukkataṃ seyyo ||  
pacchā tapati dukkataṃ ||  
kataṃ ca sukataṃ seyyo ||  
yaṃ katvā nānutappati ||  
Kuso yathā duggahīto ||  
hattham evānukantati ||  
sāmaññaṃ dupparāpaṭṭham ||  
nirayāyūpakaḍḍhati ||  
Yaṃ kiñci sithilaṃ kammaṃ ||  
saṅkiliṭṭhaṃ ca yaṃ vataṃ ||  
saṅkassaraṃ brahmacariyaṃ ||  
na taṃ hoti magapphalan ti[^050-1] || ||  

7. Idam avoca bhikkhave Tāyano devaputto ||  
idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth-ev-antaradhāyi ||  
Uggaṇhātha bhikkhave Tāyanagāthā ||  
pariyāpuṇātha bhikkhave Tāyanagāthā ||  
atthasaṃhitā bhikkhave Tāyanagāthā ādibrahmacariyikāti || ||  

SN_1.2,1.9. Candima.

1. Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati || ||  
Tena kho pana samayena Candimā devaputto Rāhunā asurindena gahito hoti ||  
Atha kho Candimā devaputto Bhagavantam anussaramāno tāyaṃ velāyam imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  

2. Namo te buddha vīr-atthu ||  
vippamutto si sabbabhi ||  
sambādhapaṭippanno-smi ||  
tassa me saraṇaṃ bhavāti || ||  

3. Atha kho Bhagavā Candimaṃ devaputtam ārabbha Rāhum asurindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi ||  
Tathāgatam arahantaṃ ||  
Candimā saranaṃ gato ||  
Rāhu candaṃ pamuñcassu ||  
dubbhā lokānukampakāti || ||  

4. Atha kho Rāhu asurindo Candimaṃ devaputtaṃ muñcitvā taramānarūpo yena Vepacitti asurindo ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā saṃviggo lomahaṭṭhajāto ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitaṃ kho Rāhum asurindaṃ Vepacitti asurindo gāthāya ajjhabhāsi ||  

5. Kinnu santaramāno va ||  
Rāhu candaṃ pamuñcasi ||  
saṃviggarūpo āgamma ||  
kinnu bhīto va tiṭṭhasīti || ||  

6. Sattabhā me phale muddhā ||  
jīvanto na sukhaṃ labhe ||  
buddhagāthābhihīto-mhi[^050-2] ||  
no ce muñceyya Candimanti[^050-3] || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 051 -->
<a id="page-051"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 051]*

10 Suriyo.

1. Tena kho pana samayena Suriyo devaputto Rāhunā asurindena gahito hoti ||  
Atha kho Suriyo devaputto Bhagavantam anussaramāno tāyaṃ velāyaṃ imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  

2. Namo te buddha vīra-tthu ||  
vippamutto si sabbadhi ||  
sambādhapaṭippanno-smi ||  
tassa me saraṇaṃ bhavāti || ||  

3. Atha kho Bhagavā Suriyaṃ devaputtaṃ ārabbha Rāhum asurindaṃ gāthāya[^051-1] ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Tathāgatam arahantam ||  
suriyo saraṇaṃ gato ||  
Rāhu pamuñca suriyaṃ ||  
buddhā lokānukampakā ti || ||  
Yo andhakāre tamasi pabhaṃkaro[^051-2] ||  
verocano maṇḍalī uggatejo ||  
mā Rāhu gilī caraṃ antalikkhe ||  
pajaṃ mama[^051-3] Rāhu pamuñca suriyan ti || ||  

4. Atha kho Rāhu asurindo Suriyaṃ devaputtaṃ muñcitvā taramānarūpo yena Vepacitti asurindo ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā saṃviggo lomahaṭṭhajāto ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhitaṃ kho Rāhuṃ asurindaṃ Vepacitti asurindo gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  

5. Kinnu santaramāno va ||  
Rāhu suriyam pamuñcasi ||  
saṃviggarūpo āgamma ||  
kinnu bhīto tiṭṭhasīti || ||  

6. Sattadhā me phale muddhā ||  
jīvanto na sukhaṃ labhe ||  
buddhagāthābhihito-mhi[^051-4] ||  
no ce muñceyya suriyan ti || ||  
Pathamo vaggo ||  
Tass-uddānaṃ ||  
Dve Kassapā ca Māgho ca ||  
Māgadho Dāmali[^051-5] Kāmado ||  
Pañcālacaṇḍo ca Tāyano ||  
Candima-Suriyena te dasāti || ||  

___________________________________________

CHAPTER II. -- ANĀTHAPIṆḌIKA-VAGGO DUTIYO.

SN_1.2,2.1. Candimaso. Sāvatthiyam ārāme || ||  

1. Atha kho Candimaso[^051-6] devaputto abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yenaḥ


<!-- PTS Page 052 -->
<a id="page-052"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 052]*

Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Candimaso devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  

2. Te hi sotthiṃ gamissanti ||  
kacche vāmakase[^052-1] magā ||  
jhānāni upasampajja ||  
ekodinipakā satā ti || ||  
Te hi pāram gamissanti ||  
chetvā jālaṃ va[^052-2] ambujo ||  
jhānāni upasampajja ||  
appamattā raṇaṃ jahā ti || ||  

SN_1.2,2.2. Veṇḍu.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Veṇḍu[^052-3] devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  

2. Sukhitā va[^052-4] te manujā ||  
Sugataṃ payirūpāsiya ||  
yuñja[^052-5] Gotamasāsane ||  
appamattānusikkhare ti || ||  
Ye me pavutte satthipade[^052-6] ||  
(Veṇḍūti Bhagavā) anusikkhanti jhāyino ||  
kāle te appamajjantā ||  
na maccuvasaṅgā[^052-7] siyun ti || ||  

SN_1.2,2.3. Dīghalaṭṭhi.

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe ||  

2. Atha kho Dīghalaṭṭhi devaputto abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo[^052-8] kevalakappaṃ Veḷuvanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Dīghalaṭṭhi devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi[^052-9] || ||  

3. Bhikkhu siyā jhāyī vimuttacitto[^052-10] ||  
ākaṅkhe ca[^052-11] hadayassānupattiṃ ||  
lokassa ñatvā udayabbayaṃ ca ||  
sucetaso asito tadānisaṃso ti[^052-12] || ||  

SN_1.2,2.4. Nandano.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Nandano devaputto Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Pucchāmi taṃ Gotama bhūripaññaṃ[^052-13] ||  
anāvaṭaṃ[^052-14] Bhagavato ñāṇadassanaṃ ||  


<!-- PTS Page 053 -->
<a id="page-053"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 053]*

Kathaṃ vidhaṃ sīlavantaṃ vadanti ||  
kathaṃ vidhaṃ paññāvantam vadanti ||  
kathaṃ vidhaṃ dukkham aticca iriyati[^053-1] ||  
kathaṃ vidhaṃ devatā pūjayantīti[^053-2] || ||  

2. Yo sīlavā paññavā bhāvitatto ||  
samāhito jhānarato satimā[^053-3] ||  
sabb-assa sokā vigatā pahīnā[^053-4] ||  
khīṇāsavo antimadehadhārī[^053-5] || ||  
Tathāvidhaṃ sīlavantam vadanti ||  
tathāvidhaṃ paññāvantaṃ vadanti ||  
tathāvidho dukkhaṃ aticca iriyati ||  
tathāvidhaṃ devatā pūjayantīti || ||  

SN_1.2,2.5. Candana.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Candano devaputto Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Kathaṃ su tarati[^053-6] oghaṃ ||  
rattindivam atandito ||  
appatiṭṭhe anālambe ||  
ko gambhīre na sīdatīti ||  

2. Sabbadā sīlasampanno ||  
paññavā susamāhito ||  
āraddhaviriyo pahitatto ||  
oghaṃ tarati duttaraṃ ||  
virato kāmasaññāya ||  
rūpasaññojanātigo ||  
nandībhavaparikkhīṇo[^053-7] ||  
so gambhīre[^053-8] na sīdatīti || ||  

SN_1.2,2.6. Sudatto

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho[^053-9] Sudatto devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  
Sattiyā viya omaṭṭho ||  
ḍayhamāno[^053-10] va matthake ||  
kāmarāgappahānāya ||  
sato bhikkhu paribbaje ti || ||  

2. Sattiyā viya omaṭṭho ||  
ḍayhamāno va matthake ||  
sakkāya diṭṭhippahānāya ||  
sato bhikkhu paribbaje ti[^053-11] || ||  

SN_1.2,2.7. Subrahmā.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Subrahmā devaputto Bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi || ||  
Niccam utrastam idaṃ cittaṃ ||  
niccam ubiggam idaṃ mano ||  


<!-- PTS Page 054 -->
<a id="page-054"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 054]*

anuppannesu kiccesu ||  
atho uppattitesu ca ||  
sace atthi anutrastaṃ ||  
taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito ti || ||  

2. Na aññatra bojjhaṅgatapasā||  
na aññatra indriyasaṃvarā||  
na aññatra[^054-1] sabbanissaggā[^054-2] ||  
sotthiṃ passāmi pāṇinanti || ||  

3. Tatth-ev-antaradhāyīti[^054-3] || ||  

SN_1.2,2.8. Kakudho.

1. Evaṃ me sutam ekam samayaṃ Bhagavā Sākete viharati Añjanavane Migadāye || ||  
Atha kho Kakudho[^054-4] devaputto abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappaṃ Añjanavanam obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten-upasaṅkami upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi || ||  

2. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Kakudho devaputto Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Nandasi samanā ti || ||  
Kiṃ laddhā āvuso ti || ||  
Tena hi samaṇa socasī ti || ||  
Kiṃ jīyittha āvusoti || ||  
Tena hi samaṇa n-eva nandasi na ca[^054-5] socasīti || ||  
Evam āvuso ti || ||  

3. Kacci tvam anigho bhikkhu ||  
atho nandī na vijjati ||  
kacci tam ekam āsīnaṃ ||  
aratī nābhikīratīti || ||  

4. Anigho ve ahaṃ yakkha ||  
atho nandī na vijjati ||  
atho mam ekam āsīnaṃ ||  
aratī nābhikīratīti || ||  

5. Kathaṃ tvam anigho bhikkhu ||  
kathaṃ nandi na vijjati ||  
kathaṃ tam[^054-6] ekaṃ āsīnaṃ ||  
aratī nābhikīratīti || ||  

6. Aghajātassa[^054-7] ve nandī ||  
nandījātassa[^054-8] ve aghaṃ ||  
anandī anigho bhikkhu ||  
evaṃ jānāhi āvuso ti || ||  

7. Cirassaṃ vata passāmi ||  
brāhmaṇaṃ parinibbutaṃ ||  
anandim anighaṃ bhikkhuṃ ||  
tiṇṇaṃ loke visattikanti[^054-9] || ||  

SN_1.2,2.9. Uttaro.

1. Rājagaha nidānam[^054-10] || ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Uttaro devaputto Bhagavato santike imaṃ gātham abhāsi || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 055 -->
<a id="page-055"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 055]*

2. Upanīyati jīvitam appam āyu ||  
jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā ||  
etaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇe pekkhamāno ||  
puññāni kayirātha sukhāvahānīti || ||  

3. Upanīyati jīvitam appam āyu ||  
jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā ||  
etaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇe pekkhamāno ||  
lokāmisaṃ pajahe santipekkho ti[^055-1] || ||  
10 Anāthapiṇḍiko.

1. Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho Anāthapiṇḍiko devaputto Bhagavato santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  
Idaṃ hitaṃ Jetavanaṃ ||  
isisaṅghanisevitaṃ ||  
āvuttham[^055-2] dhammarājena ||  
pītisaṃjananaṃ mama || ||  
Kammaṃ vijjā ca dhammo ca ||  
sīlam jīvitam uttamaṃ ||  
etena maccā sujjhanti ||  
na gottena dhanena vā || ||  
Tasmā hi paṇḍito poso ||  
sampassaṃ attham[^055-3] attano ||  
yoniso vicine dhammaṃ ||  
evaṃ tattha visujjhati || ||  
Sāriputto va paññāya ||  
sīlen-upasamena[^055-4] ca ||  
yo pi pāraṅgato bhikkhu ||  
etāva paramo siyā ti[^055-5] || ||  

2. Idam avoca Anāthapiṇḍiko devaputto ||  
idaṃ vatvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth-eva antaradhāyi || ||  

3. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi || ||  

4. Imam[^055-6] bhikkhave rattiṃ aññataro devaputto abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappaṃ Jetavanam obhāsetvā yenāham ten-upasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi ||  
Ekam antaṃ ṭhito kho bhikkhave[^055-7] so devaputto mama santike imā gāthāyo abhāsi || ||  

5. Idaṃ hitaṃ Jetavanaṃ ||  
isisaṅghanissevitaṃ ||  
āvutthaṃ[^055-8] dhammarājena ||  
pītisaṃjananaṃ mama || ||  
kammaṃ vijjā ca dhammo ca ||  
sīlaṃ jīvitam uttamaṃ ||  
etena maccā sujjhanti ||  
na gottena dhanena vā || ||  
Tasmā hi paṇḍito poso ||  
sampassam attham[^055-9] attano ||  


<!-- PTS Page 056 -->
<a id="page-056"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 056]*

yoniso vicine dhammaṃ ||  
evaṃ tattha visujjhati || ||  
Sāriputto va paññāya ||  
sīlen-upasamena[^056-1] ca ||  
yo pi pāraṅgato bhikkhu etāva paramo[^056-2] siyā ti || ||  

6. Idam avoca bhikkhave so devaputto ||  
idam vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth-ev-antaradhāyīti || ||  

7. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
So hi nūna bhante Anāthapiṇḍiko devaputto bhavissati ||  
Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati āyasmante Sāriputte abhippasanno ahosīti || ||  

8. Sādhu sādhu Ānanda ||  
yāvatakaṃ kho Ānanda takkāya pattabbaṃ anuppattaṃ[^056-3] tayā ||  
Anāthapiṇḍiko hi so Ānanda devaputto ti || ||  
Anāthapiṇḍika-vaggo dutiyo || ||  
Tass-uddānaṃ || ||  
Candimāso ca Veṇḍu[^056-4] ca ||  
Dīghalaṭṭhi ca Nandano || Candano ca Sudatto ca ||  
Subrahmā || Kakudhena ca ||  
Uttaro[^056-5] navamo vutto ||  
dasamo Anāthapiṇḍiko ti || ||
